宏观研究
宏观点评
内外需延续分化格局
——10月宏观数据点评
事件：2026 年 11 月 16日，国家统计局发布 10 月份国内宏观经济数据。10
月份，全国规模以上工业增加值同比增长 4.8%，社会消费品零售总额同比增
长 0.4%；1—10 月全国固定资产投资（不含农户）同比下降 8.1%，房地产
开发投资同比下降 21.6%。
核心观点：
 10 月国内经济内外需分化格局延续，“供强需弱”特征进一步显现。各
口径表现情况看，出口高增与新兴产业快速发展继续对工业生产形成有
力支撑，10 月规模以上工业增加值同比增长 4.8%，高技术制造业、装备
制造业增加值分别增长 17.6%和 10.6%；但内需端进一步走弱，社零受
假期错位影响增速再度放缓，10 月同比增长 0.4%、较上月回落 0.7 个百
分点，1—10 月固定资产投资同比下降 8.1%，房地产开发投资下降
21.6%，降幅较上月分别扩大 0.9和 1.0个百分点。

 往后看，伴随各项稳增长政策落地，包括新型政策性金融工具、专项债
等资金加快形成实物工作量，以及房地产因城施策与城市更新持续推进
等，后期国内经济增长动能有望边际改善。但政策力度与节奏、外需持
续性及海外货币政策变化仍是主要不确定性来源。
 风险提示：1、政策进度不及预期；2、出口回落超预期；3、美联储政策
紧缩超预期。
2026 年 11 月 18 日

1、外需与新动能继续对工业生产形成支撑
10 月份，全国规模以上工业增加值同比增长
4.8%，增速较上月的 5.5%回落 0.7 个百分点，低于
市场一致预期的增长5.3%，1—10月累计增长5.3%。
10 月外需与新动能继续对工业生产形成支撑。
以美元计当月国内出口同比增长35.2%，受此带动规
模以上工业企业出口交货值同比名义增长18.6%，外
需对工业生产的拉动作用持续显现。另一方面，10
月份高技术制造业增加值同比增长17.6%，装备制造
业增长 10.6%，分别快于全部规模以上工业 12.8 和
5.8个百分点；3D打印设备、锂离子电池、工业机器
人产量同比分别增长85.9%、30.0%和26.0%，新能源
汽车产量增长17.1%，新动能产业继续为工业生产注
入强劲动能。
图表 1 规模以上工业增加值增速（%） 图表 2 规上工业企业出口交货值增速（%）
数据来源：同花顺 iFinD、银泰证券研究所

业下降4.4%，煤炭开采和洗选业下降2.9%，非金属

下降1.7%，化学原料和化学制品制造业仅增长1.3%。

2、假期错位影响社零增速再度放缓
10月份，社会消费品零售总额同比增长0.4%，
增速较上月的1.1%回落0.7个百分点，低于市场一
致预期的0.8%，1—10月累计增长1.0%。当月社零
增速放缓，中秋假期错位带来明显扰动，但国内消费
需求偏弱同样是重要原因。
按消费类型分，10月商品零售额同比增长0.3%
（上月为+1.0%），餐饮增长0.7%（上月为+2.1%），
增速分别放缓0.7 和1.4 个百分点。10月份限额以
上单位消费品零售额同比下降4.9%，降幅较上月扩
大2.8个百分点，自4 月以来持续处于负增长区间。
主要商品类别中，汽车类同比下降21.4%（上月为
-21.2%），继续对社零整体形成拖累；此外通讯器材
类同比增长6.5%（上月为31.1%），增速较上月明显
放缓，家用电器和音像器材类增长2.5%（上月为
1.9%）边际有所改善。其它商品类别中，烟酒类增长
2.5%（上月为13.8%），饮料类增长0.3%（上月为

5.0%），文化办公用品类增长4.1%（上月为7.9%），
中西药品类增长3.7%（上月为5.0%），化妆品类增
长2.8%（上月为5.4%），增速较上月均有不同程度
的放缓。

3、固投延续调整，新动能投资较快增长
1—10月份，全国固定资产投资（不含农户）同
比下降 8.1%，降幅较 1—9 月的-7.2%扩大 0.9个百分

点，低于市场一致预期的-6.8。民间固定资产投资同
比下降 11.4%，降幅继续扩大。
分领域看，基础设施投资同比下降 5.1%，降幅
较 1—9月扩大 0.9个百分点；制造业投资下降 2.9%，
降幅扩大 0.8个百分点。
结构上看，投资向新动能集中的特征进一步强
化。1—10 月知识产权产品投资同比增长 9.2%，占
全部投资比重 16.2%，拉动全部投资增长 1.3个百分
点；设备工器具购置投资增长 8.9%，占比 20.3%、
比上年同期提高 3.2个百分点，拉动全部投资增长 1.5
个百分点。高技术产业投资同比增长 4.0%，其中信
息服务业投资增长 20.5%、航空、航天器及设备制造
业增长 10.2%、电子及通信设备制造业增长 6.6%。
基建投资落地进程偏慢，1—10月电力、热力、
燃气及水生产和供应业投资下降 7.7%（1—9 月为
-6.8%），交通运输、仓储和邮政业增长 0.7%（1—9
月为+1.9%），水利、环境和公共设施管理业下降

14.4%（1—9月为-12.8%），投资增速较上月均有不
同程度的放缓。

21.6%，降幅较1—9 月的-20.6%扩大1.0个百分点。

竣工面积下降 26.6%，开发端各环节仍全面处于低

销售端看，1—10 月新建商品房销售面积同比
下降13.7%，降幅较1—9月扩大 0.5个百分点；销

相对积极的是，二手房交易持续改善。1—10
月全国二手房交易网签面积 67971 万平方米、同比

价格环比由上月上涨0.1%转为持平，其中上海、深
圳分别上涨0.2%和0.1%，广州持平、北京下降0.1%；
二线城市环比由持平转为下降0.1%，三线城市下降
0.2%、降幅与上月相同；70个大中城市中新建商品
住宅价格环比上涨城市14个，比上月减少3 个。
二手住宅方面，一线城市环比上涨0.2%、涨幅
与上月持平（上海+0.5%、广州+0.2%、深圳+0.1%、
北京持平），二线、三线城市均下降0.3%；二手住
宅价格环比上涨城市7 个，比上月减少2 个。
同比看，一二三线城市新建商品住宅价格降幅

分别收窄0.4、0.4和0.3个百分点，二手住宅价格
降幅分别收窄1.1、0.4和0.4个百分点，一线城市
价格企稳趋势继续得到确认，但全国层面地产仍处
于筑底阶段。

Document 098: 2027年・春闘賃上げ率の見通し ～賃上げ率は４年連続の５％台か～

Source PDF: all report pdf/2027年・春闘賃上げ率の見通し ～賃上げ率は４年連続の５％台か～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026年11月 17日

1/3 
2027年・春闘賃上げ率の見通し 
～賃上げ率は４年連続の５％台か～ 
 経済調査部 シニアエグゼクティブエコノミスト  新家 義貴 
（℡：050-5474-7490）

（要旨） 
○ 2027年の春闘賃上げ率を、厚生労働省ベースで 5.15％（2026年：5.18％）、連合ベースで
4.98％（同 5.01％）と予測する。2027年春闘でもほぼ前年並みの高い賃上げが続き、厚生労
働省ベースでは４年連続で５％台が実現するだろう。連合は 2027 年春闘の要求目安を前年
よりやや引き上げ、経団連も高い賃上げの継続に前向きな姿勢を維持している。ＵＡゼンセ
ンも前年並みの高い要求水準を示すなど、労使双方の賃上げ姿勢にトーンダウンはみられな
い。 
○ 構造的な人手不足が続くなか、人材の確保・定着に向けた賃上げの必要性は高い。企業収益
も総じて堅調であり、高水準の利益が賃上げ余力を支えている。2026年の物価上昇率が前年
から鈍化することは賃上げ率の下押し要因となる。一方、春闘交渉が本格化する 2027年初に
は物価上昇率が再び高まる可能性があり、実質賃金の安定的な改善を求める労使双方の問題
意識も強い。賃上げ率が一段と加速することまでは見込みにくいものの、高い賃上げを継続
する環境は維持されている。 
○ ４年連続で５％台の賃上げが実現すれば、日本銀行にとっても金融政策の正常化に向けて前
向きな材料となる。特に、労使双方が今後も２％程度の物価上昇が続くことを前提として賃
金を設定するようになっているかどうかが、２％の物価安定目標の定着を見極めるうえで重
要となる。

2027年春闘でも前年並みの高い賃上げ率を予想 
2027年の春闘賃上げ率を 5.15％と予測する（厚生労働省「民間主要企業春季賃上げ要求・妥結
状況」ベース）。2024年春闘では5.33％、2025年では 5.52％、2026年でも 5.18％と３年連続で
５％台の高い賃上げが実現したが、2027年もほぼ前年並みの高い賃上げが続き、４年連続で５％
台となるだろう。 
春闘においては、厚生労働省よりも公表時期が早い、連合による集計値が注目されることが多
い。この連合ベースの数字では、2027年の春闘賃上げ率を 4.98％と予測する（2026年春闘：
5.01％）。５％台に乗るかどうかは微妙なところだが、こちらも歴史的にみれば高い賃上げとなる

### Page 2

2/3 
だろう。

組合側、経営側ともトーンダウンはみられず  
組合側、経営側とも賃上げに前向きな姿勢を維持している。連合は 10月22日に公表した2027
年春闘基本構想において、定昇込みの賃上げ要求を「６％中心、５％以上」、ベースアップ等の賃
上げ分を「４％中心、３％以上」とし、前年の賃上げ「５％以上」、ベア「３％以上」よりも要求
姿勢をやや強めた。経営側も賃上げの継続に前向きだ。経団連の経営労働政策委員会報告原案で
は、高い賃上げの「社会全体への浸透」が掲げられていると報じられた。また、筒井経団連会長
は 11月９日の記者会見で、ベア実施の検討を賃金交渉のスタンダードとする方針を堅持する考え
を示した。また、主要産業別労働組合組織では、ＵＡゼンセンが定昇込み６％、ベア等４％とい
う前年と同等の高い要求方針素案を示している。中東情勢悪化によるコスト上昇という逆風があ
るなかでも、労使ともに賃上げ姿勢がトーンダウンしている様子は窺えない。もっとも、連合の
要求目安は強まったものの、ＵＡゼンセンの要求率は前年と同水準であり、現時点では賃上げ率
のさらなる加速を見込むほどの材料が揃っているわけではないことには注意が必要だ。

物価は前年から鈍化も、人手不足と好調な企業収益が押し上げ要因に 
高い賃上げを支える最大の要因は、構造的な人手不足である。生産年齢人口の減少が続くな
か、企業の人手不足感は依然として強く、人材の確保・定着は重要な経営課題となっている。特
に若年層を中心に採用競争が厳しくなっており、既存人材のつなぎ止めのためにも企業は継続的
な賃上げを行う必要がある。ここ数年の春闘では、こうした人手不足を背景に高い賃上げが実現
しており、企業の賃金設定行動にも変化が生じている。2027年春闘でも人手不足を背景とした賃
上げ圧力は強いと考えられる。 
好調な企業収益も、高い賃上げを支える材料になる。中東情勢悪化に伴うコスト上昇は企業収
益の下押し要因となっているものの、価格転嫁の進展やＡＩ関連需要の拡大などを背景に、企業
収益は総じて堅調に推移しており、水準も非常に高い。企業規模や業種によって差はあるもの
の、企業収益が全体として高水準にあり、賃上げ余力は十分に確保されていると見てよい。 
一方、物価については、2026年の物価上昇率が前年から鈍化することが賃上げ率の下押し要因
になるとみられる。春闘交渉では前年の暦年の物価上昇率が参照されることが多いが、2026年の
上昇率（ＣＰＩ総合）は前年比＋２％前後と、2025年の前年比＋3.2％から鈍化する見込みであ
り、物価高への対応という観点からの賃上げ圧力は弱まる可能性がある。 
もっとも、暦年でみれば物価上昇率は鈍化する一方、春闘交渉が本格化する 2027年初には再び
高まり、ＣＰＩは前年比＋３％台に達する可能性がある。この場合、実質賃金が再びマイナスに
転じる恐れもある。そもそも、過去数年にわたって実質賃金が減少してきたことで、家計には賃
上げの恩恵を十分に実感できないという不満が残っている。実質賃金の改善の遅れへの問題意識
は組合側、経営側にも共有されており、実質賃金を安定的に増加させることは 2027年春闘でも重
要な課題となるだろう。春闘交渉の時点で物価上昇率の再加速が意識されれば、春闘賃上げ率の

### Page 3

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
3/3 
上振れ要因となる可能性もあることには注意したい。

２％「定着」判断の重要な材料に  
以上を踏まえ、2027年春闘では5.15％と、ほぼ前年並みの高い賃上げを予想する（厚生労働省
ベース）。賃上げ率が一段と加速することまでは見込みにくいものの、高い賃上げを継続する環境
は維持されていると考えられる。 
４年連続で５％台の賃上げが実現すれば、賃金上昇の持続性を重視する日本銀行にとっても前
向きな材料となるだろう。特に重要なのは、労使双方が今後も２％程度の物価上昇が続くことを
前提として賃金を設定するようになっているかどうかである。2026年の物価上昇率が前年から鈍
化するなかでも高い賃上げが維持されれば、こうした賃金設定が広がりつつあることを示す一つ
の材料となる。日銀は基調的な物価上昇率が２％の物価安定目標とおおむね整合的な水準に達し
たとの認識を示したが、２％の物価安定目標が「定着」したとまでは判断していない。2027年春
闘は、その定着を見極めるうえでも重要な判断材料となるだろう。

漢唐(2404 TT) Buy

3Q26 獲利優於預期，客戶建廠需求暢旺，給 Buy 的投資建
議。 
投資建議： 3Q26 獲利優於預期，加上在手訂單金額約 1,939 億
元，有助於未來漢唐營運，給 Buy 的投資建議。 
漢唐 3Q26 獲利優於預期：漢唐 3Q26 營收 275.06 億元，
QoQ+5.47%；稅後純益 45.69 億元，QoQ+21.43%；稅 後 EPS 23.97
元。3Q26 稅後純益優於原先預估的 39.27 億元，主因毛利率優
於預期。 
漢唐目前在手訂單金額約 1,939 億元：漢唐 01~05/2026 年接單
合約金額約 674 億元，截至 05/2026 年已簽約未認列之金額(在
手訂單)約 1,939 億元。在手訂單約 24~36 個月可以認列大部分。
目前在手訂單金額的施工地區，有 2/3 在台灣，1/3 在海外。 
24~36 個月認列大部分的在手訂單可能是保守估計，2H26 在手
訂單金額可能還會再增加。 
漢唐對營運樂觀：漢唐表示目前建廠需求遠大於供給，因目前
人力短缺，尤其是在台灣，暫時沒辦法去接其他大型客戶的訂
單。由於 AI 與相關需求，現在客戶建廠的需求會延續到 2029
年，漢唐對整體產業的展望表示樂觀。

2 群益投顧 
  ESG：  
永續環境是漢唐經營理念之綠色承諾，因應全球環保節能的需求，我們透過
工程設計、系統整合提升工程效率，減少工程過程中能耗。漢唐集成響應政
府綠色採購辦法，我們從原物料取得、工程施作、運輸配送、完工至客戶使
用階段，優先要求廠商提供低污染、可回收、再利用之環保產品，以達到廢
棄物減量之目標。 
漢唐集成根著台灣，從初創之施工安裝小公司成長為專業工程集成公司，有
賴於各地政通人和，因此，漢唐集成本著感恩的心回饋在地，不斷捐助弱勢
及支持培育英才，期望肩擔起企業之「社會責任」，讓台灣變得更美好。漢
唐股東會由全體股東組成，對公司重大事項進行決策，定期聽取董事會報
告，為公司最高決策機關；董事會為最高治理機關，董事會成員皆恪盡善良
管理人之注意義務，擘劃公司的經營政策與檢視財務績效，並確保公司營運
遵守各種法令；為完善公司治理運作、強化公司競爭力，董事會設置審計委
員會與薪資報酬委員會來健全董事會運作；董事會之下亦設有獨立之稽核
室，定期執行稽核業務並向審計委員會與董事會呈報稽核結果。 
公司簡介： 
漢唐集成成立於 1982 年，為一以「全面性整合服務」方式，專業提供高科
技產業建廠之系統集成服務公司；主要業務範圍包括高科技廠房各系統，包
括電腦、控制、無塵室、機電、電信系統之施作與整合服務。 
漢唐目前在手訂單金額約 1,939 億元： 
漢唐 01~05/2026 年接單合約金額約 674 億元，截至 05/2026 年已簽約未認
列之金額(在手訂單)約 1,939 億元。在手訂單約 24~36 個月可以認列大部
分。目前在手訂單金額的施工地區，有 2/3 在台灣，1/3 在海外。 
24~36 個月認列大部分的在手訂單可能是保守估計，2H26 在手訂單金額可
能還會再增加。 
漢唐未來還有先進封裝廠的訂單： 
大客戶於美國建廠的第三期工程的訂單目前漢唐已經有接到一部份，還沒
有認列到手訂單金額中，客戶的先進封裝廠的訂單還沒發包。第二期工程進
度希望在 2026 年認列約 85%，第三期工程的認列狀況要看客戶，未來還有
先進封裝廠的訂單。 
美國營收已超過台灣： 
漢唐沒有透露前兩大客戶的營收占比。2025 年營收占比_地區別：台灣 65%、
美國 25%、其他 9%。 1Q26 台灣 40%、美國 56%、其他 4%。美系記憶體廠
在台灣蓋無塵室，漢唐還是主要的供應商。 
漢唐對營運樂觀： 
漢唐表示目前建廠需求遠大於供給，因目前人力短缺，尤其是在台灣，暫時
沒辦法去接其他大型客戶的訂單。由於 AI 與相關需求，現在客戶建廠的需
求會延續到 2029 年，漢唐對整體產業的展望表示樂觀。

3 群益投顧 
其他補充： 
漢唐的費用比較固定，管理費用包含員工酬勞。漢唐追求與客戶長期合作，
如果是原物料上漲導致成本增加，客戶可以接受漲價。 
目前漢唐在全球的員工人數約 1,700 人，美國、台灣都會再增加人力，之後
也是要看下包廠商，看是否能擴大供應鏈。

6 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】 
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首次評等潛在上漲空間<15%

中立(Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等 
預期近期股價將處於盤整 
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】 
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。  
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」、「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。 
停止推薦情境： 
1. 達目標價。 
2. 雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。 
3. 推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】 
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Wiwynn (6669.TW): ASIC AI servers and General servers to drive growth 
ahead; 3Q26 and Oct in line; Buy
13 November 2026 | 8:50PM HKT
Wiwynn Oct revenues were in line with our estimates at NT$159bn, driven by solid 
general servers growth and its major ASIC customers’ new generation of ASIC AI 
servers (air-cooling) entering mass production since June 2026. Looking ahead, we 
model sequential revenue increase in Nov and Dec, considering liquid-cooled ASIC AI 
servers of its major customers, and as we expect GPU AI server racks ramp up in 
4Q26E. We expect 4Q26 revenues growth to remain strong, up to +15% QoQ/ 71% 
YoY, accelerating further to +38% QoQ in 1Q27, driven by AI servers’ new models 
ramp up, AI servers’ expansion from ASIC to GPU, potential ASIC AI servers SKU 
expansion, and less impact from the general server Buy & Sell to Consignment shift. 
Maintain Buy with TP unchanged at NT$4,000 (19.2x 2027E target PE vs. +37% EPS 
YoY in 2027-28E).

3Q26 results in line: Revenues is pre-announced; GM declined to 8.9%, in line with 
our estimates, while better than Bloomberg consensus of 8.4%, leading GP to +51% 
QoQ, in line with our estimates, or 13% ahead of Bloomberg consensus. The lower 
GM reflects AI servers ramp up, along with less NRE as new models entering mass

production. Meanwhile, opex ratio was down to 1.5%, largely in line with our /

Earnings revision: We factor in 3Q26 results and Oct revenues, and change our net 
income by 1% / +6% / +0% in 2026-28E. The change is mainly on revenues and opex 
ratio: (1) we raise 2026-27E revenues by 2% / 2%, reflecting the ramp up of new AI 
server products, and the company’s potential of new ASIC AI servers customers 
expansion. (2) We cut 2026-27E opex ratio by 0.1 ppts/ 0.2 ppts, reflecting improved 
operation efficiency as the company expands its AI servers. We keep our 2028E 
estimates largely unchanged.

Valuation: We continue to derive our 12M TP based on near-term P/E, and our 2027E

our positive view on Wiwynn’s leading position in ASIC AI server ODM along with product

### Page 4

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Wiwynn 
We are Buy-rated on Wiwynn with a 12-month TP of NT$4,000. Our target price is 
based on 19.2x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E 
multiple correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s performance. 
Key downside risks: 1) weaker-than-expected AI server demand growth; 2) 
slower-than-expected recovery in general server demand; and 3) more severe 
competition in the server ODM market.

Source: Company data, Goldman Sachs Research estimates, FactSet. Price as of 13 Nov 2026 close.

Realtek Semiconductor  | Asia Pacific
Asia Pacific Summit 2026
Feedback
Realtek remains relatively resilient despite sluggish PC demand, supported by its
diversified product portfolio and platform agnostic strategy. Near-term customer
inventory adjustments appear to reflect shipment timing rather than underlying
demand weakness. Meanwhile, elevated legacy DRAM prices continue to pressure
device BOM costs, potentially delaying broader adoption of edge Al and other

consumer applications
Looking ahead, edge server and edge Al represent structural growth opportunities,
supported by ongoing development in high-speed SerDes and integrated Al
solutions. Realtek also sees opportunities to strengthen its position in higher-end TV
SoCs amid competition. A normalization in memory prices could provide an
additional catalyst for downstream demand recovery.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Realtek Semiconductor (2379.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We assume a cost of equity of 9.6%, an intermediate growth
rate of 7.3%, payout ratio of 79%, and terminal growth rate of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected Wi-Fi growth
n Faster-than-expected new product growth
n Faster-than-expected adoption for auto Ethernet
n Fading PC semi inventory corrections
Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected Wi-Fi growth

n Slower-than-expected new product growth
n Slower-than-expected margin recovery given ongoing pricing competition
n Further TV SoC project losses in China
n Slower-than-expected adoption for auto Ethernet

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

### Page 1

ab 19 November 2026
Global Research
Gudeng Precision Industrial
Broad-based 3Q beat sets up a solid 2027
Solid Q3 earnings beat; momentum continuing into Q4 and 2027
Gudeng’s Q326 results came in above consensus, with revenue 5% and EPS 16% above
VA consensus. The beat was broad-based, driven by stronger revenue, gross margin, and
opex leverage. We expect momentum to continue into Q426, with full-year 2026
revenue growing 34%, vs our prior estimate of 29%, driven by continued strength in

EUV and China FOUP. Q4 gross margin should sustain at 46% level, with better margin
of EUV mask pod. Looking into 2027, we estimate revenue growth of 31%, primarily
volume-driven, with upside if Gudeng can add more capacity for FOUP. Of note,
advanced packaging revenue could potentially reach NT$0.85bn, up meaningfully from
~NT$0.3bn in 2026. Below the line, we estimate FY27 gross margin of 46%, with
favorable mix from EUV and China FOUP, while the opex ratio should improve to 24%

from 25% in 2026.
EUV mask pod sales to grow 30% in 2027
EUV demand has remained consistently strong throughout 2026 and the sales should
grow 43% this year and 30% in 2027. Key drivers are the meaningful leading edge
expansion at TSMC, and Gudeng’s share gain in Intel. We estimate Gudeng’s EUV mask
pod shipment to Intel to likely close to triple YoY this year. DUV mask pod sales should
also grow 15-20% in 2027, supported by stable demand from China logic customers.

Meaningful recovery in China FOUP lifting ASP and gross margin
On top of the EUV strength, the business recovery in China FOUP is equally important in
re-accelerating Gudeng’s earnings and margin improvement. Gudeng has seen strong
demand from more diversified client base across logic and memory YTD, versus in the
past when growth was more reliant on tier 1 logic clients like SMIC. Considering the
tight supply at global peers, we anticipate the growth of China FOUP to sustain and

support 40% foup revenue growth in 2027. On TSMC side, we believe Gudeng should
also be improving product mix through 2027 with the upgraded diffuser wafer foup
with higher ASP and margin for N2. On advanced packaging, Gudeng is set to ramp up
sales more meaningfully from NT$300mn this year to NT$0.85bn in 2027, mostly
through large panel FOUP for substrate makers, but also good volume for CoWoS
related FOUP.
Valuation: Maintain Buy with lower price target of NT$610 from NT$710

Post Q3 results, we upgraded 2026/27 EPS by 14% and 2%. We lower our price target
from NT$710 to NT$610 based on a lower multiple of 26x 27-28E PE from 30x
previously, considering the broader sector de-rating and lower LT earnings CAGR of
26% from 29%. Maintain Buy.

Company Description
Gudeng Precision Industrial was founded in 1998 and entered semiconductor front-end
process supply chain in 2000. It manufactures reticle and wafer carriers,which provide
protection, delivery and storage solutions; it also supplies related equipment. According to

the company, it is the largest supplier of reticle carriers with a 70% market share globally and
holds an 85% market share for EUV POD, while its FOUP is adopted by over 50% of
customers in Greater China as the baseline.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Gudeng based on PE methodology.
Downside risk for the semis industry includes: 1) slower-than-expected N2 migration, 2)
geopolitical uncertainties that weigh on the semis cycle, and 3) meaningful deceleration of

new technology deveopment like Cloud AI, autonomous driving etc.
Downside risk for the company itself includes: 1) slower-than-expected share expansion in
FOUP market, 2) executions on pushing new products like diffuser FOUP, high NA EUV pods.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113)金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

融程電(3416)：國防軍工引領成長 
建議：維持買進

一、融程電為強固型電腦廠商，
近年國防軍工營收大幅提升 
 融程電成立於 1996 年，公司前三大股東為研華、醫揚、廣積。融程電為強固型
平板電腦和手持式裝置專業廠商，產品廣泛應用於航太、船舶、軍警、交通運輸、
車廠維修檢測、醫管、石化、和製程控制等利基型產業；歐洲為融程電最大市場，
賓士、保時捷、Bosch 等皆為融程電客戶，美洲市場方面，智慧倉儲解決方案廠
商 Honeywell、堆高機品牌 CROWN 以及智慧醫療 Medtronic 等亦是融程電客
戶。近年國防軍工營收大幅提升，自 2024 年的高個位數佔比提升至 2025 年的
近兩成水準，2026 年已突破兩成水準；截至 3Q26 Edge AI 佔比約 10%。

二、3Q26 毛利率低於預期，係
因出貨較多倉儲客戶產品 
 3Q26 營收 14.13 億元，截至 3Q26 產品組合：車檢 21%、倉儲物流 23%、醫
療保健 12%、其他 44%(其中超過 25%為國防軍工)；獲利方面，毛利率 36.36%，
低於預期，係因 3Q26 出貨較多低毛利率 Honeywell 倉儲物流產品，營益率
19.05%，單季稅後 EPS 2.63 元。

三、2027 年仍由國防軍工驅動主
要成長 
 3Q26 毛利率受 Honeywell 倉儲物流產品出貨較多影響，Honeywell 2026 全年
訂單金額預估 2~3 億元，2027 年 Honeywell 目標回到往年全年 6 億營收水準，
目前仍在與客戶洽談新訂單；4Q26 軍工航太及車用等出貨比重提升，預期毛利
率較 3Q26 回升；整體預期 2026 全年營收+30%YOY，其中出貨量成長 10%，
其餘 20%來自 ASP 提升(包含漲價與產品組合轉佳)。 
融程電目前 BB Ratio 1.1，訂單能見度約 6~8 個月，由於缺料狀況緩解，客戶
下單能見度恢復正常水準，預期 2027 年受惠軍工航太、無人載具及 Edge AI 需
求成長，產品組合持續優化將推升 ASP 及毛利率。產品組合方面，截至 3Q26
其他業務營收佔比 44%，2027 年佔比有望提升至 50%，主由國防軍工驅動，
2027 年國防軍工預期再成長 30~40%，甚至有望上看超過 50%成長，融程電具
全球領先海事船舶電腦技術，拓展至地面工作站及無人機整機，無人載具現有月

6415 矽力杰( Silergy Corp.)

報告日期：2026/11/16          研究員：王蕎程(Joe Wang) 
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要: 
 本業獲利持續改善：3Q26 營業利益季增 29.7%，營益率提升
至 13.26%；惟 2Q26 受金融資產評價利益挹注，墊高比較基
期，第三季獲利下滑主要來自業外收益回落，並非本業衰退。 
 AIDC 與車用雙引擎帶動成長：AIDC 營收年增逾 120%，新一
代 SPS 預計 2027 年開始放量；車用受惠 MCU 及高壓大電流
產品導入，帶動單車價值提升，預期 2027 年兩大業務營收均
有望成長逾 50%。 
 製程升級與供貨優勢支撐成長：Gen4 產品持續放量，預期 2027
年底營收占比達 50%，搭 配 12 吋產能布局及 Gen5 新品量產，
有利爭取大型客戶，惟封測產能吃緊及成本上升仍為毛利改善
的觀察重點。

一、3Q26 本業獲利持續改善，預估 4Q26 EPS 2.5 元 
矽力*-KY 為電源管理 IC 設計廠，3Q26 營收 72.59 億元（QoQ 
+20.9%、YoY +52.4%），主要受惠 AIDC、工業及車用需求成長，
以及消費電子客戶提前拉貨。毛利率 47.40%，QoQ +0.4 個百分點，
主要因產品組合改善；營業利益 9.63 億元（QoQ +29.7%），營業
利益率 13.26%，QoQ +0.88 個百分點，本業獲利持續改善。惟 2Q26
受金融資產評價利益 9.59 億元挹注，墊高獲利基期，3Q26 因業外
損益回落至 1.11 億元，稅後淨利降至 9.09 億元（QoQ -42.1%），
EPS 2.34 元，獲利下滑主要來自業外收益減少，並非本業衰退。
展望 4Q26，消費電子需求受第三季提前拉貨及終端市場疲弱影
響，手機、電視等產品出貨轉弱，惟資料中心需求仍維持成長，
預估營收 74.63 億元，毛利率 47.80%，稅後 EPS 2.50 元。

二、產品組合優化，AIDC 與車用為明年主要成長動能 
AIDC 為公司主要成長來源，3Q26 營收占比達 17%，YoY +120%。
隨 AI 伺服器 CPU 及 GPU 功耗提高，主板供電系統需要處理更大
電流，同時降低電力損耗及散熱負擔，帶動高效率電源管理 IC 需
求。其中，新一代 SPS（Smart Power Stage）將功率元件、驅動器
及感測功能整合於單一封裝，可改善高電流供電效率並縮小電路
板空間，目前已進入多家客戶驗證階段，預期 2027 年開始貢獻營
收。公司表示，北美客戶對相關產品的需求較為迫切，預期放量
速度將快於中國客戶；此外，伺服器主板、資料中心通訊設備及
SSD 為 AIDC 主要成長應用，三者合計可占 AI 相關營收約 70%～

三、車用單車價值提升，光通訊與 12 吋製程布局 
車用方面，公司持續增加單一車款的晶片供應種類，除既有電源
管理產品外，高壓、大電流電源 IC、MCU 及電池管理相關產品陸
續導入域控制器、電驅等應用，帶動單車產品價值提升。公司表
示，高階車款單車產品價值預計由 2026 年底的 200～220 美元，提
升至 2027 年的 270～300 美元；海外新客戶近期已開始小批量出
貨，明年貢獻可望增加，惟中國客戶仍為主要營收來源，預期 2027
年車用營收成長逾 50%。光通訊方面，隨 AI 資料中心傳輸速率提
升，光模組對供電效率、功耗及散熱的要求同步提高，帶動高效
率電源轉換、雷射驅動電源及整合式電源管理產品需求。公司已
布局 NPO 及 CPO 相關產品，並進入客戶設計導入階段。

製程方面，公司布局 12 吋晶圓產能，目前的產能可支應至少 20
億美元營收規模。隨 AIDC 及車用需求增加，類比 IC 供應鏈產能
趨緊，部分同業仍以 8 吋製程為主，矽力則因 12 吋產能準備較為
充足，有利爭取重視供貨穩定性的大型客戶。公司表示，Gen4 產
品持續放量，預期 2027 年底營收占比約 50%，Gen5 則預計於 2027
年量產。新製程除改善成本結構外，也能支援 AIDC 對電源轉換效
率、功率密度及產品整合度的要求，有利提升高階產品比重。

供應鏈方面，目前主要瓶頸在封裝測試，受設備供應不足及裝機
速度限制，產品生產週期由原先約 4 個月延長至 9 個月，公司須
提前備妥晶圓等待後段排產，帶動在製品增加。預期 2027 年上半
年封測產能仍難明顯改善，較大幅度的產能紓解可能落在 2027 年
下半年至 2028 年。雖然公司前段晶圓供應相對充足，但後段產能
限制仍可能影響出貨速度，且成本上升未必能完全轉嫁客戶。

四、預估 2026 / 2027 年稅後 EPS 10.87 / 14.72 元 
預估 2026 年營收 255.85 億元（YoY +36.0%），毛利率 47.58%，營
業利益 31.33 億元，稅後 EPS 10.87 元。展望 2027 年，受惠 AIDC
及車用新品放量、Gen4 良率改善與高階產品比重提升，預估營收
319.81 億元（YoY +25.0%），毛利率提升至 50.07%；營業利益 57.83
億元（YoY +84.6%）。考量業外收益回歸正常水準，預估稅後 EPS 
14.72 元（YoY +35.4%）。

綜合考量：(1) AIDC 需求持續成長，帶動伺服器主板、SSD 及光

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一. 公司簡介 
矽力杰股份有限公司（簡稱：矽力 KY；代號：6415）主要從事類
比、數位混和 IC 之研發設計及銷售，產品應用範圍廣泛，主要
應用於 3C 產業、雲端伺服器、工業類、汽車、5G 通訊等相關
領域，包括 LED 照明、平板電腦、筆記型電腦、固態硬碟、視
頻監控、伺服器、數位機上盒、智能 音箱、智慧型手機、多媒體
電視、LED 背光模組、路由器、行動電源、汽車電子、通訊基地
台及智能電錶等，並持續增加各種終端應用，如 TV Panel、手機
快充、醫療領域等。

矽力 KY 目前產品主要是類比 IC 晶片，產品廣泛應用於消費性
電子產品、工業用產品、資訊產品、5G 網路通訊產品及汽車電子
產品。針對終端產品設計需求，該公司可提供不同的晶片，例如
平板電腦、電池充電管理晶片、直流對直流轉換晶片、過流保護
晶片、靜電防護裝置、LED 背光驅動晶片以及 PMU 等；針對 LED 
照明，該公司可提供 LED 照明驅動晶片；針對機上盒，該公司
可提供交流對直流轉換晶片，直流對直流轉換晶片，過流保護晶
片，EMI 防護裝置等；智慧電表，則提供包含能源監控管理(Energy 
measurement IC)的系統晶片 System on Chip (SOC) 以及高精度電
流檢測方案。

二. 產業趨勢概況 
市場概況 
(1)半導體市場概況與 IC 設計業 
半導體產業為工業類設備、資訊電腦、網路通訊、消費性及汽車
電子產品應用的關鍵性產業，亦是目前主導性的基礎產業之一，
隨著 3C(電腦 Computer、通訊 Communication、消費 Consumer)產
品的蓬勃發展和工業類產品智慧化和低能耗的需求，產品生命週
期縮短而更加快速，並朝多元化及豐富化發展，隨著產品推陳出
新與成長，均促進未來半導體需求及市場穩定發展。國內半導體
產業鏈發展日益成熟，有完整的上、中、下游分工體系，使得 IC 
設計公司得以專精於從事設計工作，再將 IC 的製造委託給晶圓
代工製作成晶圓半成品，經過前段測試後再交由封裝廠進行切割
及封裝，最後再經專業測試廠做後段測 試後，完成最後產品，也
造就了良好的 IC 設計產業發展環境。 IC 元件一般可分為數位
邏輯 IC 及類比 IC 兩大類。數位 IC 利用 0、1 非連續性方式
傳遞訊號，多用於處理數位資訊如計算、控制、儲存等，強調尺
寸、速度與功耗(Process Driven)。類比 IC 則負責處理來自自然界
的連續性類比訊號，如光、 熱、電、速度、壓力等物理量，並作
為橋接上述各類物理訊息與數位電子系統的媒介，需同時藉由製
程、電路設計和半導體元件物理的相互配合，在晶片效能及成本 
上尋求最適化，由於其決定了數位產品所能呈現的最終呈現品

### Page 5

公司研究報告

條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保證。非經本公司同意，不得
將本報告加以引用或轉載。 
5 
質，因此更為注重元件的特性(Design Driven)，琢磨於可靠度、穩
定度、功率消耗、能源轉換效率、電壓電流控制能力等

(2)電源管理 IC 
隨著環保與節能議題發酵，節能科技為目前重要發展課題之一，
而類比 IC 中 的電源管理 IC 正扮演著不可或缺的關鍵性角
色，電源管理 IC 主要作為在不同的系統運作狀態下，隨時控制
並保持系統內適當電流與電壓正常供給。而電源供應器的好壞，
將會直接影響電子產品的使用壽命。近年來電源供應器均朝向輕
薄短小、省電、耐用的方向發展。而要產品達到這些目標的重要
關鍵，乃在於如何把電源供應器做到高效率(高效能)、高穩壓率和
低雜訊干擾，這其中所有參與管制功能的 IC 即被稱為「電源控
制、管理類比 IC」，每年市場均保持穩定成長，消費電子、5G 網
路通信、移動互聯領域、雲端伺服器、工業應用都是目前最主要
市場，未來並朝向人工智慧及電動車及車連網方向發展。電源管
理單元(PMU)是一種高度集成的電源管理方案，將傳統分立的若干
類電源管理器件整合在單個的封裝之內，可實現更高的電源轉換
效率、更低功耗，以及更少的元件數。PMU 在有限的體積內集成
了盡可能多的電源通道，根據各個子單元的需求、提供高效率的
供電，或提供低雜訊的乾淨電源，又或者是系統必須的上電時序
控制，成為為系統供電的首選。過去十年智慧型手持式裝置在市
場快速發展，總體來看，終端市場整體還將保持快速成長，在此
大背景下，PMU 的市場需求有望實現較之前市場預期更高的發展
態勢。隨著智慧型手持式產品的大螢幕化、輕薄化，電池的體積
也將會越來越小，在相應的電池容量不變的情況下，要求整機的
續航能力要更強，除了不斷降低晶片功耗改善以外，對電源的智
慧化管理也變得不可或缺，因此未來 PMU 市場還會有不錯發展。

未來展望 
電源管理 IC 是類比 IC 最基礎和重要的產品，近年來電源管理
一直是半導體領域的熱門市場之一，且電源管理 IC 是所有電子
產品必備的關鍵零組件，屬於高功率類比電路技術，主要用途為
提供電子產品穩定且適當電壓的電源，將直接影響電子產品的使
用壽命。隨著全球 3C 產品的功能不斷增加，汽車自動駕駛和工
業自動 化需求，終端應用漸漸走向低耗電、輕薄短小與多功能整
合以及對產品壽命與可靠度要求更高的趨勢，為達到此目的，電
源管理 IC 必須做到使電源系統效率提升、節能及縮小 IC 元件
封裝以減少所佔用之電路板面積等重點。另隨著 CPU 不斷向上 
躍進，對電源的穩定性及電壓的精準度也就更為要求。此外，高
整合度不但能減少零件數量，達到降低系統耗電以及提升系統可
靠度及品質等效益，並可提高生產良率，且降低生產工時，進而

降低成本。而隨著環保與節能的議題發酵，節能科技為目前重要
發展課題之一，電源管理 IC 扮演不可或缺的關鍵角色。除低耗
電、極低的靜態消耗電流等需求外，低耗電使用無鉛封裝製程亦
是全球各國對環保、低耗能等綠色概念的要求重點，而電源管理 
IC 同時也得考量未來環保法規帶來的限制，故符合環保及綠色能
源將成為電源管理 IC 設計的重要方向。

叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

一、預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 2.50 元 
矽力*-KY 3Q26 營收 72.59 億元（QoQ +20.9%、YoY +52.4%），受
惠 AIDC、工業及車用需求成長，毛利率 47.40%，營業利益 9.63
億元（QoQ +29.7%）；惟 2Q26 金融資產評價利益墊高比較基期，
3Q26 業外收益回落，稅後淨利 9.09 億元（QoQ -42.1%），EPS 2.34
元。展望 4Q26，消費電子需求轉弱，惟資料中心需求維持成長，
預估營收 74.63 億元（QoQ +2.8%），毛利率 47.80%，稅後淨利 9.76
億元，EPS 2.50 元。

二、預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 10.87 元 
預估 2026 年營收 255.85 億元（YoY +36.0%），受惠 AIDC 及車用
需求增加，毛利率 47.58%，營業毛利 121.74 億元；營業利益 31.33
億元，營業利益率 12.25%，業外損益 15.70 億元，稅後淨利 42.24
億元，稅後 EPS 10.87 元。

三、預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 14.72 元 
展望 2027 年，受惠 AIDC 新一代 SPS 產品導入、車用單車價值提
升，以及 Gen4 製程良率改善與高階產品比重增加，預估全年營收
319.81 億元（YoY +25.0%），營業毛利 160.12 億元，毛利率 50.07%；
營業利益 57.83 億元（YoY +84.6%），營業利益率 18.08%，業外
損益 4.51 億元，稅後淨利 57.34 億元（YoY +35.7%），稅後 EPS 14.72
元。

### Page 1

기아 (000270)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 자동차/로봇 | 2026.11.02  
잠시 쉬어갑니다

3Q26 Review 
기아는 3Q26P 매출액 31.6조원(+10.1% YoY, -4.4% QoQ), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.4% YoY, -30.8% QoQ, OPM 5.8%)을 기록, 컨센서스를 하회했다. 영업이익 증
감 요인은 환율 효과(+5,290억원), 물량 효과(+4,420억원), 믹스 효과(-4,650억원), 
인센티브/가격 효과(-7,270억원), 재료비 영향(-3,680억원), 기타(+4,130억원)이다. 
판매 성장에도 높아진 수익성 부담 
글로벌 도매 판매 84.7만대(+7.9% 이하 YoY), 중국 제외 판매량은 83.1만대
(+8.8%)를 기록했다. 미국 HEV와 서유럽 EV 판매가 각각 118%, 163% 증가하며 
글로벌 점유율은 4.0%까지 확대됐다. 다만, 판매 성장에도 원화 강세와 원자재 가격 
상승, EV 비중 확대에 따른 믹스 부담으로 수익성은 전분기 대비 하락했다. 유럽 내 
EV 판매가 늘어나면서 믹스가 악화되었으며, 전쟁으로 인한 원자재 상승으로 3분기 
재료비 부담은 전년 대비 약 3,700억 증가했다.  
연간 판매목표 335만대는 유지했으나, 4분기 수익성의 급격한 반등은 어려울 전망이
다. 향후 미국 텔루라이드·스포티지 HEV 공급 확대와 유럽 EV 판매 증가에 따른 고정
비 흡수가 수익성 회복의 핵심이다. 
투자의견은 Buy를 유지하고 목표주가는 155,000원으로 하향 
기아에 대한 투자의견 Buy를 유지하고, 실적 추정치 하향을 반영해 목표주가를 
155,000원으로 하향한다. 재료비 부담, 환율 효과 축소, 예상보다 큰 믹스 악화로 단
기 이익 눈높이 조정은 불가피하다. 다만 산업수요 둔화에도 주요 시장에서 점유율을 
확대되고 있다는 점은 긍정적이다. 현재 배당 수익률은 7% 수준으로, 상대적으로 높
은 배당 매력은 주가 하방을 지지할 전망이다.

(원) 주가 목표주가

Document 220: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명SK하이닉스(000660) HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 - 기업

### Page 1

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
90 
SK하이닉스  (000660) 
2026.11.04

HBM 하나로 시가총액 설명 
[반도체/전기전자] 고의영 
2122-9179 ey.ko@imfnsec.com

업황 우려에 고유 할인까지 겹친 과도한 하락 
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 230만원으로 커버리지를 개시한다. ‘27년 BPS에 목표 
P/B 2.3배를 적용했다[표1]. 이는 상장 이후 평균 P/B의 +2표준편차에 해당한다. 
‘26~’28년 ROE는 각각 102%, 66%, 47%로, ‘26년 정점 이후에도 과거 업사이클 
고점보다 높은 수준이 유지될 전망이다.  
한편, 이익 지속성에 대한 믿음이 공고해져 시장의 시야가 ‘28년으로 향할 경우 
‘28년 BPS 기준 목표주가는 360만원까지 열릴 수 있다[표2]. 
동사 주가는 ‘26년 6월 고점 대비 10월 저점 기준 57% 하락해 과거 다운사이클 
평균 하락폭인 -51%를 넘어섰고, 과거 8~13개월에 걸쳐 진행됐던 조정이 
이번에는 4개월 만에 나타났다. 시장이 ‘28년 이익 급감을 상정하고 있는 것이다. 
그러나 당사는 아래와 같은 투자포인트와 향후 주가 흐름을 제시한다.

‘28년 HBM 가치, 시가총액의 1.3배 
HBM 비중이 높아질수록 동사의 이익 변동성은 낮아질 것이다. 특히 cHBM으로 
갈수록 고객별 수주생산 제품으로 현물시장과 유통 재고가 없고, 생산에 앞서 
물량과 가격이 결정된다. 동사는 이 전환이 업계에서 가장 선두권이다.  
당사는 동사 HBM 매출이 ‘26년 43조원에서 ‘28년 183조원으로 증가할 것으로 
전망한다. 만약, 동사의 ‘26~’28년 HBM 영업이익에 시기별 TSMC P/E(21배, 
15배, 12배)를 적용하면 HBM 가치는 동사 시가총액의 47%, 93%, 135%를 
설명한다[표3]. ‘27년에 이미 HBM 하나만으로 현재 시가총액이 설명된다는 
의미다. 시장이 통상적으로 받아들이는 멀티플 10배를 타겟팅해도 결과는 크게 
다르지 않다.

150만원에 사면 ‘30년 EPS 2배 
동사의 주주환원은 자사주 매입/소각 중심일 것으로 상정한다. 당사는 동사가 현 
정책 기간(‘25~’27년)에 이어 ‘28년 이후에도 FCF의 50%를 환원하고 이 중 
70%를 자사주 매입/소각에 쓸 것으로 가정했다.  
당사 가정대로라면 총주주환원수익률은 현 시가총액 대비 ‘27년 14%, ‘28~’30년 
연 17~24%다[표4]. 
관건은 매입 단가다. 자사주 매입의 주당가치 개선 효과는 P/E가 낮을수록 
커지는데, 현 주가의 ‘27년 P/E는 2.7배다. 현 주가 수준인 150만원에 매년 균등 
매입한다고 ‘30년까지 주식수는 50% 줄어든다.   
그 결과 ‘30년 주당순이익은 매입이 없을 때 대비 2배가 된다[표4]. ‘28년 이후 
이익이 정체되더라도 주당 이익은 계속 늘어나는 구조이며, 이익 지속성에 대한 
의심이 주가를 누를수록 소각 효과는 커진다. 현 주가 수준의 정체가 장기화되기 
어려운 이유다.

### Page 2

반도체(Overweight): Stress Test  
91

‘27년 주가 경로: 회복, 박스권, 재평가 
연말까지는 급락 이후 저점을 높여가는 회복 구간이다. 솔리다임 상장 철회에 이어 3분기 
실적발표에서 ‘26년 주주환원 100조원과 매 분기 자사주 매입 방침이 제시되면서 환원 
의지는 검증되었다. 
‘27년 상반기까지는 ‘28년 불확실성에 대한 우려가 상존할 전망이다. 가격 하락폭은 LTA 
방어력이 확인되기 전까지 블랙박스다. 이 불확실성이 멀티플을 누르는 동안 ‘27년 이익은 
계속 상향되므로 EPS 상승과 멀티플 하락이 상쇄되는 구간이 이어질 전망이다. 이 구간의 
기회비용은 환원으로 보상받는다. 
‘27년 하반기 이후에는 실적 추정의 기준 연도가 ‘28년으로 넘어가고, 신규 팹 물량이 소량 
출하되면서 센티먼트상 우려는 커질 것이다. 그러나 당사는 2H27에 ‘28년 HBM 협의와 
LTA 연장이 지속적으로 확인되면서 이익이 스트레스 테스트 범위 안에서 유지될 것으로 
전망하며[그림2], 이것이 숫자로 확인되는 시점에 순환 할인이 축소되고 재평가가 나타날 
것으로 판단한다.

업사이드 요인 
① AI 투자 지속성 우려로 최근 주가 하락이 가팔랐던 만큼, 이 부분에 대한 우려가 
완화되면 반등의 속도와 폭이 예상보다 빠를 수 있다 
② 향후 주주환원 규모가 예상보다 더 적극적일 경우 추가적인 업사이드가 될 수 있다. 
혹은 주주환원 이후에도 남는 대규모 FCF의 활용 방안도 촉매가 될 수 있다.

Compliance notice 
당 보고서 공표일 기준으로 해당 기업과 관련하여, 
 회사는 해당 종목을 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 금융투자분석사와 그 배우자는 해당 기업의 주식을 보유하고 있지 않습니다.  
 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

SK하이닉스 (000660)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G는 +11.5%, ASP는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

### Page 6

SK하이닉스 (000660)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

TUẦN 02-06/11 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -16.26 điểm tương ứng -0.93%, với thanh 
khoản mở rộng

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
Diễn biến phân hóa và biến động mạnh có thể còn tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu 
hướng tăng ngắn hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ vọng sẽ tiếp tục

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -16.26 điểm tương ứng -0.93%, với thanh khoản mở 
rộng. Thiết bị dụng cụ Y tế, Tiện ích khí và Bảo hiểm là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt 
nhất trong tuần qua. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị 
-870 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã MBB, VIC và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
ở mức 504 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng -2,010 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua 
và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -10,888 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 1,208 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -4,070 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam có tuần giảm điểm thứ 2 liên tiếp khi VN-Index lùi về kiểm định vùng hỗ trợ 
quanh 1.725 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm giảm hơn 30 điểm trước khi thu hẹp 
đà giảm nhờ lực cầu ở nhóm chứng khoán. Áp lực bán tập trung ở nhóm vốn hóa lớn, đặc 
biệt là bộ đôi Vingroup (VIC, VHM) cùng một số cổ phiếu ngân hàng và công nghệ, khiến 
chỉ số thiếu lực đỡ. Giao dịch của khối ngoại diễn biến trái chiều và giằng co — có phiên 
mua ròng trở lại ở một số mã, nhưng vẫn xen kẽ bán ròng ở nhóm trụ như VHM, VCB, cho 
thấy dòng vốn ngoại chưa xác lập xu hướng rõ ràng. Bối cảnh bên ngoài kém thuận lợi khi 
nhóm cổ phiếu công nghệ tại Mỹ bị bán tháo, kéo theo các chỉ số chính toàn cầu đồng 
loạt lao dốc. Mặc dù vậy, kỳ vọng vào động thái mở kênh hoán đổi ngoại tệ của NHNN để 
hỗ trợ thanh khoản, hạ lãi suất đã giúp tâm lý nhà đầu tư trong nước ổn định và không 
xuất hiện áp lực bán tháo.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến spinning thân nhỏ với bóng trên/dưới khá dài, 
cùng thanh khoản gia tăng, thể hiện sự giằng co mạnh nhưng về tổng thể lại cho thấy diễn 
biến đã cân bằng trở lại sau tuần giảm điểm trước đó. Trên khung ngày, mặc dù trải qua 
các phiên biến động tăng/giảm mạnh đan xen nhưng việc cho phản ứng bật tăng tích cực 
tại quanh đường biên dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn cho thấy tương quan 
vẫn đang nghiêng về chiều hướng tăng. Diễn biến phân hóa và biến động mạnh có thể còn 
tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ tiếp tục hướng lên thử thách lại các vùng cản phía trên.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Document 331: European Economics Analyst_ UK Outlook 2027_ Cyclical Headwinds, Strengthening Fundamentals

Source PDF: all report pdf/European Economics Analyst_ UK Outlook 2027_ Cyclical Headwinds, Strengthening Fundamentals.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-11-15
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

The UK economy proved surprisingly resilient in 2026. Growth accelerated n
despite rising energy prices and tighter financial conditions, and the labour 
market showed signs of stabilisation. The new government navigated a difficult 
fiscal backdrop to deliver a low-key budget that maintained the consolidation 
path. 
The coming quarters are likely to be more challenging. First, we expect growth to n
slow as the impact of the energy shock on consumer spending increases over the 
winter. Second, inflation will rise further as electricity and gas bills increase and 
the indirect effects of the energy shock build. We expect this to push the BoE to 
hike a second time, probably in February. Third, the government faces a difficult 
Spending Review; existing spending plans look too tight, but the Chancellor has 
limited fiscal headroom. 
Nonetheless, we see compelling reasons to be constructive on the economic n
outlook for 2027. First, some of the recent growth improvement looks structural, 
and the productivity trend is strengthening. We therefore expect activity to 
reaccelerate later in the year as some of the cyclical headwinds fade, and our 
Q4/Q4 growth forecast is above consensus at 1.4%. Second, we do not expect 
strong second-round effects from the energy shock into domestic inflation, so 
the BoE is likely to hike significantly less than priced. Third, we expect further 
fiscal consolidation, and a more favourable OBR forecast could ease the 
trade-offs facing the Chancellor. 
We also see scope for sentiment around the UK’s longer-term economic n
prospects to improve. The UK is well-placed to benefit from AI adoption given its 
strengths in professional services, and surveys indicate that companies expect a 
material boost to productivity growth in the coming years. The PM has also 
recently raised the possibility of closer EU ties, which could bring notable 
economic gains over time.

### Page 2

UK Outlook 2027: Cyclical Headwinds, Strengthening Fundamentals

The UK economy proved surprisingly resilient in 2026. Despite the energy shock keeping 
inflation elevated and causing the BoE to pivot from cutting to hiking, growth 
accelerated this year. The labour market showed signs of improvement, with 
unemployment falling and employment growth indicators ticking up. The new 
government navigated a challenging fiscal backdrop to deliver a low-key Budget that 
maintained the consolidation path.

Cyclical Headwinds 
The coming quarters are likely to prove more challenging in several respects. First, we 
expect growth to slow in the near term and run slightly below trend in Q4 and the first 
half of 2027. The impact of the energy shock on household finances is likely to build over 
the winter, with weak real disposable income growth resulting in a slowdown in 
consumer spending.

Exhibit 1: Growth Proved Surprisingly Resilient in 2026

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bank of England
15 November 2026   2
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 3

Tighter financial conditions should also weigh on growth as mortgages are refixed at 
higher rates. Fiscal policy will likely turn more contractionary compared with this year 
given that current plans imply a larger consolidation in the primary balance in 2027.

While the labour market data improved over the last few months, we expect slower 
near-term growth to result in a modest further weakening. That is consistent with 
continued softness in most indicators of employment intentions. The pace of loosening 
should be gradual as the peak impact of last year’s labour cost increases on employment 
growth has probably passed. But we see the unemployment rate rising back to 5% by the 
middle of next year.

Exhibit 2: Consumer Spending Growth Likely to Slow

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics

Exhibit 3: Growth Headwinds from Rising Mortgage Rates and Tighter Fiscal Policy

Note that student loan figures for 2026-27 are affected by the accounting impact of a freeze in repayment thresholds, so the figures excluding student loans may 
give a better guide to the impacts on demand.

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bloomberg, OBR
15 November 2026   3
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 4

Second, inflation is likely to increase further in the coming months. Consumer energy 
prices will rise notably in January on the back of higher electricity and gas bills. We also 
expect food and core goods inflation to accelerate as the indirect effects of the energy 
shock become more apparent. We also see renewed upward pressure in some regulated 
services prices that are indexed to headline inflation, and higher fuel costs will likely raise 
transport services prices. Overall, we expect inflation to peak at 4.3% in February and 
average 4.1% in 2027H1.

The peak in headline inflation will likely exceed the threshold at which the BoE has 
typically become more concerned about second-round effects. Taken together with 
hawkish recent commentary, that points to another 25bp BoE hike to 4.25%. This will 
probably come in February, although an earlier December move is also possible.

Exhibit 4: Renewed Labour Market Softness

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bank of England

Exhibit 5: Rising Inflationary Pressures Point to a Second BoE Hike

The forecasts shown on the left are conditional on GS forecasts for energy prices.

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bank of England
15 November 2026   4
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 5

Third, the government faces a difficult Spending Review. Existing plans imply a notable 
slowdown in departmental spending growth from next year onwards, which looks 
challenging to deliver given pressures on the health and defence budgets. History 
suggests that these plans will probably be notably upgraded. New policy priorities could 
require further outlays, with PM Burnham’s agenda likely to become clearer after the 
publication of his 10-Year Plan. But financing higher spending may be challenging given 
limited fiscal headroom, with revenues constrained by Labour’s manifesto not to raise 
taxes on working people. Welfare reforms could bring some savings over time but look 
likely to be accompanied by upfront investment in employment support.

Structural Improvement 
Despite these cyclical challenges, we see several reasons to be more constructive on the 
UK’s economic prospects for 2027. First, some of the tailwinds that supported growth 
this year look structural. Strength in business investment has partly reflected rising 
information and communications capex, which looks likely to be sustained by ongoing 
data centre investment. This investment should support continued growth in technology 
services output, where demand has increased on the back of AI adoption. Further 
growth in utilities capital spending linked to renewables should also continue to support 
demand.

Exhibit 6: A Challenging Spending Review

Source: OBR
15 November 2026   5
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 6

The productivity trend also appears to be improving, as growth in output per worker has 
increased significantly recently. Some of this may reflect the impact of last year’s 
employer National Insurance hike on hiring, but more recent data also show gains in 
sectors such as professional services that were less affected by labour cost increases. We 
also see more fundamental reasons to expect a pickup. While Brexit has probably 
weighed significantly on growth over the past decade, the worst of the headwind has 
likely passed. Survey data from the Decision Maker Panel indicate that early gains from 
AI adoption could also have modestly boosted the figures.

These factors, together with declines in energy prices, should mean that growth 
reaccelerates through the second half of next year. Overall, we expect a 1.4% Q4/Q4 
increase in GDP, above consensus expectations as well as the BoE and OBR forecasts.

Exhibit 7: Some Growth Drivers Look More Structural

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Yotzov et al. (2026)

Exhibit 8: Trend Productivity Growth is Probably Improving

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Various Academic Studies
15 November 2026   6
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 7

Second, we continue to expect limited second-round effects into domestic price 
pressures. Even after the recent decline in the unemployment rate, broader measures of 
utilisation (such as the vacancy/unemployment ratio) suggest that the labour market 
remains far looser than in 2022/23. That will likely reduce the extent to which higher 
headline inflation passes through into wage pressures. Indeed, early data indicate that 
pay settlements are likely to remain stable next year, and the BoE’s Decision Maker Panel 
survey shows little change in wage expectations since the start of the Middle East 
conflict. Private sector regular pay growth will probably accelerate from low levels but is 
likely to remain well below the levels seen after the previous energy shock.

With only a modest increase in pay growth, we expect overall labour cost inflation to be 
well below the levels seen in recent years. This is especially true in consumer-facing 
sectors, which saw sharper increases in wages over recent years and the largest impact 
from the employer National Insurance increases. More moderate labour cost inflation

Exhibit 9: Our GDP Forecast is Slightly Above Consensus

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bloomberg, Bank of England, OBR

Exhibit 10: Second-Round Effects Likely to be Moderate

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, Haver Analytics, Bank of England
15 November 2026   7
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 8

should help to keep a lid on underlying services inflation, despite cost pressures from 
higher energy and food prices.

Given moderate second-round effects, we think that the BoE will hike less than priced. 
After tightening policy in February, we expect the MPC to keep Bank Rate at 4.25% for 
the rest of the year, whereas the market implies two further rate increases. The risks 
around our baseline are skewed towards further tightening, but even on a 
probability-weighted basis our forecast is notably below current market pricing.

Third, 2027 is likely to see renewed progress on deficit reduction. The current fiscal 
plans imply a steep reduction in the primary deficit next year. While we think spending 
could overshoot the official projections, tax receipts could also come in somewhat 
higher. Further consolidation should mean that the UK continues to compare favourably 
to other advanced economies in terms of reducing borrowing. While the Spending 
Review does pose a challenge for the government, it is possible that changes in the

Exhibit 11: More Limited Labour Cost Increases Should Keep a Lid on Underlying Services Inflation

### Page 9

economic forecast could help the Chancellor. The OBR’s wage growth forecast continues 
to look low, and an upgrade would boost the tax revenues projection, increasing fiscal 
space.

A Positive Long-Run Outlook 
We also see scope for sentiment around the UK’s longer-term economic prospects to 
improve. The UK is well-placed to benefit from AI adoption given its specialisation in 
high-value added services. IMF estimates indicate that a relatively high share of the 
workforce is exposed to AI, with a particularly large number of workers in roles that may 
see productivity gains but are less likely to be displaced. Survey data suggest that firms 
expect adoption to result in sizeable productivity increases in the coming years. Even 
though these gains are unlikely to be fully additive to the current productivity trend, the 
numbers still indicate upside risks to our estimates for supply growth.

Exhibit 13: Continued Fiscal Consolidation

On the left, the UK data are shown on a public sector basis.

Source: Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research, OBR, Bank of England
15 November 2026   9
Goldman Sachs European Economics Analyst

### Page 10

The UK is also well-positioned in several high growth innovative industries. This includes 
a world-leading position in life sciences, such as pharmaceuticals, biotechnology, 
medical technology, and genomics. The UK has a world-class research base, with the 
London/Oxford/Cambridge triangle a leading biotech cluster. 
Another possible source of long-run growth upside would be closer ties with the EU. The 
upcoming (November 20) summit could see some progress, with the two sides expected 
to finalise agrifood and emissions trading deals. But the Prime Minister has also 
indicated that he will use the summit to outline options for how the relationship might 
develop after the next election, potentially including a customs union, rejoining the 
single market, or even a second referendum on membership. Any reset would require 
negotiation, political trade-offs, and time before benefits materialise. But it could bring 
significant economic upside, so signals towards improved trading links could start to lift 
optimism on the UK’s growth prospects.

### Page 1

The prospect of faster Fed tightening and continued dislocation
in tech stocks is sucking funds into the dollar and the yen.
Popular FX carry trade positions are being unwound across the
board.  Given that a hawkish Fed lies at the heart of the
current correction in risk assets, and there are no signs of
Fed rhetoric softening anytime soon, USD can stay strong

Chris Turner, Francesco Pesole and Frantisek Taborsky
The prospect of faster Fed tightening is starting to weigh heavily on risk assets
and helping the dollar
USD: The Waller correction
After another day of losses, the US S&P 500 index is now 8.5% oﬀ
its highs of the year. The extent of this correction is not far from that
seen in March this year (9.5%) and way oﬀ the 'Liberation Day' rout
of 21% seen in March/April 2025. The cause of the current correction
has been the threat of a faster Federal Reserve tightening cycle, put
on the table by the Fed's Christopher Waller last week. We
discussed high US real interest rates here yesterday. And the
volatility in tech stocks may also be owing to this week's Anthropic

IPO, where investors have probably been shuﬄing portfolios to
participate in a $100bn IPO. The fact that the Fed wants to tighten
faster because of a demand shock should not necessarily be bad
news for equities and, barring some major earning misses, the case
for another 10% correction in equities is a weak one.
Yet there seems no reason for the Fed to drop its hawkish rhetoric
anytime soon. Next Wednesday sees the release of the Fed's
preferred inﬂation measure – the core PCE index. The October data

upside risks, and conﬁrm that the Fed will need to take action.
For FX, that means a continued strong dollar and continued
pressure on popular carry trades. The Latam complex has had a
terrible week, with the most popular carry position, the Colombian
peso, oﬀ heavily. Brazil yesterday stepped in with FX intervention,
but $1bn in sales was quickly overwhelmed. Elsewhere, the big drop

Australia next year on the view that the Reserve Bank of Australia
has overtightened. We highlighted this as one of the key risks in our
2027 FX Outlook. Pressure may also be building on long positions in
the Turkish lira, where implied yields are starting to rise again.
A big carry trade unwind and emerging markets sell-oﬀ remain a

positive for the dollar, and to a lesser degree, the yen. We expect the
dollar to hold gains into next week and keep 104.60 as a near-term
DXY target.
The US data calendar is light today, and the only Fed speakers are
the hawkish Waller (10:15CET) and Lisa Cook, who surprisingly
voted for a hike at the October FOMC meeting.

GBP: New EU-UK agreements announced
At a press conference in Brussels today, UK Prime Minister Andy
Burnham and EU leaders are expected to announce three new
agreements. The ﬁrst is on SPS (Sanitary and Phytosanitary
measures), which will reduce border checks on trade in food,

animals and plants. The second is on a greater alignment between
the separate EU and UK Emissions Trading Schemes (ETS), where
greater recognition of each other's schemes can reduce carbon-
related friction in trade – especially important for the steel and
manufacturing sectors. The third looks at the thorny issue of
mobility in the Youth Experience Scheme (YES), where up to
100,000 18-31 year-olds can obtain work visas for up to three
years. Burnham will be marketing these deals as addressing the

cost-of-living crisis and giving young Britons valuable opportunities
overseas.

Subject: GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Central Banks Are Tightening

GLOBAL MARKETS CALL: Central Banks Are Tightening

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
GLOBAL MARKETS CALL:
Central Banks Are Tightening
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 15 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Gaël Gaborel - OrbisTerrae / Unsplash

### Page 2

The global AI trade should make a nice comeback now that the latest
issue of The Economist sees the bottom falling out of it:
The weekly financial magazine, famous for its contrarian cover stories,
also recently picked the top in global bond yields when it ran a cover story
titled "Will bonds blow up?" in the October 10-16 issue. Then again, bond
yields rose worldwide last week. Central bankers sounded more hawkish,
and inflation remains sticky. Bond investors expect more monetary policy

tightening ahead.

### Page 3

Stock markets barely moved last week. Global profits are booming, yet
valuations keep falling, almost everywhere. We think they are at or near a
bottom. Gold continues to falter, and China’s problems are depressing
stock prices there. Here’s more:
I. Stock Market Derby
The US has held its ground in November. The US MSCI’s share of the All
Country World MSCI’s market capitalization is 65.3% (chart). The Europe
MSCI’s share is 13.5%. The Emerging Markets (EM) MSCI’s share has

slipped to 11.0%.
The US MSCI’s share of global forward earnings hasn’t kept pace. It has
edged down to 54.1% from 54.3% two weeks ago, well below its market-
cap share (chart). The EM MSCI’s earnings share has climbed to 20.6%
even as its market-cap share has fallen. Investors are pricing the South
Korea and Taiwan MSCIs at historically low multiples.

### Page 4

The Eurozone and UK ETFs have gained on the US ETF in recent months
(chart). The All Country World ex-US ETF continues to track the US ETF
closely. The US has pulled back less from its recent high.
Global stock markets have gone mostly sideways this month (chart). The
All Country World ETF is flat mtd. The US is in the middle of the pack,
down 0.2%. Turkey leads with a 9.0% gain. South Korea (-6.6%) and
Taiwan (-3.7%) remain at the bottom of the performance list.

### Page 5

II. Stock Market Earnings & Valuation
US and overseas forward earnings continue to climb together to record
highs (chart). The two have tracked each other closely since 2024.
Global profits are booming. The US MSCI’s forward profit margin has risen
to a record high of 16.9% (chart). The Canada MSCI’s is even higher at
17.1%. The EM MSCI’s forward profit margin has jumped to 13.9%. And
the All Country World ex-US MSCI’s is up to 12.9%.

### Page 6

Valuations have been falling everywhere. The US MSCI’s forward P/E is
down to 18.5 (chart). The EMU MSCI’s is 14.3, the Japan MSCI’s is 14.1,
and the UK MSCI’s is 12.0. The EM MSCI trades at a forward P/E of just
8.4. We think global valuations are at or near bottoming. Further downside
looks limited to us.
III. Bonds
Bond yields rose this week on hawkish central bank commentary and
stubborn inflation (chart). The 10-year US Treasury yield jumped to 5.16%

from 4.86% a week earlier. The UK’s 10-year government bond yield of
5.34% remains the highest in the group.

### Page 7

The spread between French and German 10-year yields widened to
154.2bps (chart). That’s below its October high. France’s increase this
week was much bigger than those of other European countries.
Two-year government bond yields are well above central bank policy rates
(chart). These yields are pricing in continued global tightening for the
foreseeable future.

### Page 8

IV. Gold
Gold’s latest correction resembles its pullbacks in the mid-1970s and
during the Great Financial Crisis (chart). Those corrections occurred
during or right after recessions.
Gold has had a disappointing year so far. The nearby futures price broke
below support at $4,000 per ounce and is now $3,879.70 (chart). That's
well below its 200-day moving average. We don’t see what might lift the
gold price in the near term. We are pushing back our $5,000 target to the

end of 2027. The main bullish driver is central bank gold buying. The main
bearish factor is rising interest rates.

### Page 9

V. China
The China MSCI stock price index has been falling since late last year
(chart). Forward earnings has been rising slowly, while the forward P/E
has declined more quickly to about 10.0.
China’s property bust keeps getting worse. The Shenzhen Real Estate
Stock Price Index has fallen to 897.61, further below the 1,000 support
that had held since 2012 (chart). On October 18, we wrote that we would
worry if it broke below that level. We remain concerned. Exports continue

to carry the economy, while domestic consumption remains very weak.

### Page 10

Chinese tech stocks took another hit last week. The China Technology
ETF (CQQQ) fell 4.4% (chart). Nvidia CEO Jensen Huang resigned on
November 12 amid reports linking him to chip shipments to China. Alibaba
stock fell 14%, and Baidu stock fell 4.6% last week as the news hit.
Investors fear Washington will further tighten restrictions on China’s
access to Nvidia’s processors. We continue to favor the EM ex-China
MSCI over the China MSCI.
💡

featured in our segment, "Ed Answers Your Questions".

Document 383: National Bank of Hungary review_ The doves aren’t gone yet

Source PDF: all report pdf/National Bank of Hungary review_ The doves aren’t gone yet.pdf

### Page 1

The National Bank of Hungary kept rates unchanged in
November, but delivered a dovish twist. A rate cut was already
on the table, and improving domestic risk sentiment is creating
more room for manoeuvre. Nevertheless, we only see the next
cuts coming in late 2027 as inﬂation pressures rebuild

Peter Virovacz and Zoltán Homolya
The National Bank of Hungary, Budapest
5.50% Key interest rate

ING's policy view: A dovish twist, but no
rush

### Page 2

The National Bank of Hungary kept the base rate unchanged at
5.50% on 17 November, in line with widespread expectations. This
marked the third consecutive meeting with rates being kept on
hold. While the latest communication revealed somewhat more
room for monetary easing, we think the window of opportunity will
remain narrow.
Looking ahead, the exceptionally favourable domestic inﬂation
backdrop is likely to fade relatively quickly. We see inﬂation

accelerating to 3.0% by December 2026, with repricing at the start
of next year temporarily pushing price pressures closer to 3.5% in
the ﬁrst quarter of 2027. Inﬂation could then approach 4% during
the summer, largely due to base eﬀects. In our view, this
increasingly challenging inﬂation proﬁle means that any near-term
opportunity for easing may prove short-lived.
In the meantime, however, we see the latest dovish shift in
communication potentially testing the waters and gauging market

sensitivity to eventual easing, particularly in a global environment
where hawkishness still dominates. The key litmus test will be the
forint: a muted FX reaction to the dovish rhetoric would give the
central bank greater conﬁdence that some easing could be
delivered without jeopardising market stability. This means that a
possible one-and-done December rate cut has become a clear
dovish risk to our baseline.
However, we believe that any near-term window for easing would

be relatively narrow. As 2027 progresses, the main constraint on
monetary policy is likely to shift gradually from external risks
towards the domestic inﬂation outlook. With inﬂation expected to
move closer to 4% during the summer, we see little room for a
sustained easing cycle over the next six to nine months. Any near-
term easing would therefore be more likely to represent an
opportunistic move than the beginning of a conventional cutting
cycle.

A more durable easing window could open later in 2027, once
inﬂation starts to decelerate more convincingly after the summer. If
this coincides with a benign external environment and a further
Your privacy is important

our Cookie policy.

### Page 3

decline in the risk premium on Hungarian assets, we see room for
one or two rate cuts in the fourth quarter of 2027. Our baseline
therefore sees the base rate reaching 5.00% by end-2027, followed
by a further reduction to 4.50% by end-2028.
Our three takeaways from the presser

### Page 4

Review Policy rate NBH National Bank of Hungary
Monetary policy Hungary
1. A cut was already on the table. The biggest dovish surprise
came during the press conference. The Monetary Council
considered two options: keeping rates unchanged or cutting
them, while the decision itself was not unanimous. This
suggests that the bar for easing may already be lower than
previously thought. The December Inﬂation Report will now be
particularly important, with Governor Mihály Varga explicitly

stating that favourable developments in Hungary's risk
assessment and inﬂation outlook, together with reduced
external uncertainty, could increase its room for manoeuvre.
2. Hungary's risk story has improved. Perhaps the most notable
change in the narrative is the assessment of Hungary's risk
premium. Varga highlighted the favourable market reception of
the government's ﬁscal plans and its commitment to euro
adoption as factors supporting Hungary's risk assessment and

increasing room for monetary policy. The forint has also
remained stable and is stronger than a year ago, helping to
contain inﬂationary pressures. In other words, domestic
ﬁnancial market developments are becoming less of a
constraint on monetary policy than the Monetary Council
previously considered them to be.
3. The external environment still demands caution. The greater
room for manoeuvre does not mean that the NBH is ready to
embark on an easing cycle. Energy price volatility, elevated

global yields and above-target inﬂation in major economies
continue to warrant caution. At home, inﬂation is also expected
to accelerate from its current 2% level due to fuel prices, tax
changes and other policy measures. This leaves the Monetary
Council in a familiar data-dependent position: its room for
manoeuvre has increased, but whether it can actually use that
room will depend on both the external environment and the
December inﬂation outlook.

Document 397: Q3 Wage Price Index_ award wage boost (2026-11-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Q3 Wage Price Index_ award wage boost (2026-11-23).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 14
Status: ok

### Page 1

#wages #inflation #labour
Q3 Wage Price Index: award wage boost
By Ryan Wells
Economist, Westpac
16:06 November 18 2026 Share
The minimum wage and award decision lifted wages growth, but unevenly – enterprise
arrangements responded more than individual arrangements, consistent with a softer
labour market. Q3 2026: 1.1%qtr, 3.5%yr.
The Wage Price Index (WPI) rose 1.1% in Q3 2026, slightly above Westpac’s forecast and the
market consensus for a 1.0% gain. That increased the annual growth rate to 3.5%yr – stronger than

### Page 2

Wages by Bargaining Stream
the past six months, but a similar pace to 2025.
Today’s data also showcased the impact of the 2026 Fair Work Commission’s Annual Wage Review,
which handed down a larger-than-expected 4.75% increase in award wages. This lifted wages in
aggregate, but the gains were uneven.
A significant share of the increase was driven by health care and social assistance, reflecting pay
rises for state government workers covered by enterprise agreements.

In original terms, the WPI lifted 1.7% in Q3. Awards and enterprise arrangements accounted for 64%
of the lift, while individual arrangements contributed just 36%, the smallest share for Q3 since 2019.
Enterprise arrangements, which are often directly linked to award outcomes, were more responsive,
while individual arrangements were less so compared to previous September quarters. That is
consistent with a softer labour market and recent business survey evidence that suggests the rise in

private sector labour costs following the award decision quickly normalised.
Overall, we see the Q3 result as a step up in the level of wages from the award decision rather than
evidence of building underlying wage pressure but we will be watching whether the strength in
enterprise bargaining persists.

### Page 3

Westpac has long believed focusing on wage inflation by wage bargaining stream can help to
understand how wages respond to the economic cycle. Australia’s labour market is split into three
categories of pay setting methods:
Australian wage adjustments, including the Fair Work Commission’s (FWC) minimum wage and award
rate decisions, tend to occur at the start of the new financial year in July. The FWC handed down a
4.75% increase for 2026, larger than the decisions from 2025 (3.50%) and 2024 (3.75%), but lower

than in 2023 (5.75%).
In original terms, the WPI lifted 1.7% in Q3 2026. Of that increase, 0.50ppts were attributed to awards,
which was broadly in line with our expectations. However, both individual and enterprise arrangements
surprised us to the upside, contributing 0.64ppts and 0.65ppts to the quarterly increase in wages
respectively.
National minimum wage and award rates, regulated by the Fair Work Ombudsman,
Enterprise agreements, or
Individual arrangements.

### Page 4

Compared to the same quarter in previous years, each stream's contribution was the strongest in
absolute terms since 2023, largely because the overall lift was bigger than in 2024 and 2025. After
accounting for the size of the increase, enterprise arrangements were responsible for 64% of the lift,
whereas individual arrangements contributed just 36%, the smallest share since 2019.
While the larger award wage decision pushed up the overall increase, it did not flow through evenly.

Many enterprise agreements are directly linked to award outcomes, either through explicit reference to
award rates or because the award decision sets a benchmark in bargaining, which helps explain why
this stream was more responsive. By contrast, wages set under individual arrangements can be more
discretionary and depend more on employers' circumstances, so they were less responsive than in
previous years. That may reflect a softer labour market, where weaker demand for workers reduces the

pressure on employers to lift pay.
Wages by Sector
By sector, private wages (excl. bonuses) rose 1.0% in Q3, pulling up the annual pace from 3.1%yr to
3.4%yr. This marks the strongest pace of wages growth in the private sector since 2023, at the time of
the Fair Work Commission’s decision to raise award wages by a much larger 5.75%.
Private sector pay is set through a mix of arrangements, including a significant share of individual
arrangements (as discussed above). Today's result suggests this year's award wage decision may

have had some flow-on effect to the broader private sector, though, as noted, the pass-through was
uneven and appears to have been stronger for enterprise arrangements than for individual
arrangements.
There was also an element of both breadth and scale when it comes to private sector wage gains. Half
of all private sector jobs recorded a wage increase in Q3, the largest share since this series began in
2016; and of those jobs that recorded an increase, average hourly wages rose 4.5%, the strongest

since Q3 2023 (5.8%).
Public wages continue to outpace the private sector, this time with a 1.3% lift in Q3, bringing the annual
growth pace to 3.9%yr, the strongest since Q4 2025. As expected, the bulk of the gain was driven by
pay rises under enterprise agreements at the state government level, particularly for front-line workers.
Additionally, given scheduled rises under existing APS-wide enterprise agreements for Commonwealth
employees had already been delivered in previous quarters, the Commonwealth level contribution to

public sector wages growth during Q3 was modest.
Relative to the private sector, the driver of public sector wage gains looks to be more about scale than
breadth – across the 38% of public sector jobs recording a wage increase during the quarter the
average hourly wage change was 4.0%, slightly under the record high of a 4.3% recorded back in Q4
2023.

### Page 5

Summary
The Q3 result shows that the larger minimum wage and award rate decision boosted wages but the
detail suggests the lift was driven more by workers with stronger bargaining power. Awards and
enterprise arrangements, where pay outcomes are more directly tied to the Fair Work Commission’s
decision or to collective bargaining, whereas individual arrangements, which are more exposed to
labour market conditions, contributed the smallest share since 2019.

That is consistent with a softer labour market – the unemployment rate has risen ½ppt since the start of
the year, and we are expecting it to peak at 5.2% mid next year. The NAB business survey points the
same way, with the increase in labour costs reported by private sector firms following the award
decision quickly normalising. This suggests the Q3 result is better read as a temporary step up in the
level of wages from the award wage decision than as evidence of building underlying wage pressure,

though we will be watching whether the strength in enterprise bargaining persists.

Document 457: 『主な意見』に「主な異見」なし

Source PDF: all report pdf/『主な意見』に「主な異見」なし.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 11 月 10 日(火)

1/3 
『主な意見』に「主な異見」なし    
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・10月金融政策決定会合における『主な意見』が公表された。注目は、先行きの利上げ見通し
もさることながら、浅田統一郎委員と佐藤綾野委員の物価と金融政策運営に関する認識であ
る。政策金利を1.25％に引き上げた９月会合において浅田委員と佐藤委員は双方とも反対票
を投じていた。浅田委員は「足もとの消費者物価（除く生鮮食品）の上昇率が２％を下回る
なか、経済の状況も必ずしも強いとはいえない点を踏まえると、金融市場調節方針を据え置
くことが望ましい」、佐藤委員は「経済・物価情勢が、現時点ではこれまでと比べて大きく
加速している状態にあるわけではないと考えられるもとで、このタイミングでの利上げは適
切ではない」と述べていた。こうした認識に変化がないなら、基調的な物価上昇率が２％に
達したとの認識に異論を唱えるのが自然であると考えられるが、10月会合で示された展望レ
ポートの内容は全員一致であったことが植田総裁の記者会見で明らかになっている。

・これらを踏まえたうえで、『主な意見』における物価や金融政策運営に関する記述をみる
と、浅田委員と佐藤委員のものと思われるものがあったが、物価については下振れリスクを
殊更に強調するものはなく、慎重な声としては下記の４つがあった。浅田委員と佐藤委員の
他にも２つ、物価の下振れ方向のリスクに対する言及（と読める）記述があった点はやや意
外であった。 
・日経平均株価は先行き 12 ヶ月 72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き 12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を 12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに 2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は 4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
「来年の賃上げ率が本年と比べて大きく上昇する可能性は低く、現在の物価上昇の賃金への二
次的波及のリスクは高くない。むしろ、政策要因で低下している消費者物価が、今後の賃金
交渉に与える影響を確認していく必要がある」

「足もとのインフレ率は既往の原油高を背景に上昇しているが、現時点では二次的な波及効果
は限定的であると考えている。今後、賃金の動向なども踏まえながら、基調的な物価上昇率
が『物価安定の目標』と整合的な水準で定着していくかどうかを慎重に確認する必要があ
る」

「直近の消費者物価の前年比が２％台に入る中、基調的な物価上昇率が今後２％の目標水準を
超えて上昇するリスクがあると認識しているが、一方で、既往の利上げがラグを伴って今後
経済・物価を下振れさせるリスクにも留意する必要がある」

「基調的な物価上昇率がゼロ％近傍から上昇してきたわが国では、２％へのアンカー度合いが
欧米対比弱いため、外生的なショックに対する反応が大きくなる懸念がある」

・他方、金融政策運営については浅田委員と佐藤委員のものと思われる記述があり、何れもハ
ト派であった。ただし、次回以降の利上げに「必ず」反対票を投じるというほどの強い語気
は感じられない。

「引き続き政策金利を引き上げる前提のもと、慌てて中立金利の水準に引き上げなくても、基
調的な物価上昇率は先行きも概ね２％程度に定着するものと考えられる。ヘッドラインの物
価が一時的に乖離したとしても、２％に収束するメカニズムが働くことについて、対外的に
説明していくことが必要である」

「金融環境はまだ緩和的であるが、緩和の度合いは徐々に縮小している。政策金利の水準も中
立金利の推計レンジに入っており、今後の利上げが経済、ひいては基調的な物価上昇率を押
し下げるリスクに十分留意する必要がある」

・３～４ヶ月に１度の利上げが「慌てて」に該当するかは微妙であるが、次回12月会合で利上
げの提案があった場合、この（一つ目の）記述の主が反対票を投じても不思議ではない。そ

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
れでも、政策委員会全体としては「基調的な物価上昇率が２％の『物価安定の目標』と概ね
整合的な水準となる中、現在の金融環境が緩和的であることを踏まえると、経済・物価・金
融情勢に応じて、引き続き政策金利を引き上げ、金融緩和の度合いを調整していくことが適
当である」との考え方で一致している。筆者は引き続き、12月会合における利上げを予想す
る。もっとも、世界的にＡＩ・半導体株の調整が長引くようだと、2027年１月まで待つ選択
肢も浮上するとみている。

大型銀行3Q26獲利成長延續，但幅度收斂，主要
是非利息收入表現開始降溫，多數銀行認為投行案
源充足，需求只是遞延而非消失，債權相關業績因
利率走高而下滑，但股權相關業績仍有收獲；相比
之下，3Q獲利貢獻焦點一致轉向了淨利息收入，
業績增長得益於貸款與存款穩定成長，而財富管理
業務也提供了顯著的成長。部份銀行非利息費用成
長幅度大於收入成長幅度，導致效率比率變差。儘
管信貸品質仍健康無虞，但仍有部份銀行對潛在風
險展現謹慎態度，因而提高壞帳準備金。
展望後市，因淨利息收入的前景相對清晰，故可見

《摩根大通：企業投資與消費穩健，高利率紅利延
續》
3Q26營收年增8%、每股盈餘年增10%。淨利息收
入年增13%，受存款、信用卡及企業貸款成長帶動；
非利息收入年增3%；市場收入年增15%，由股票交
易大增帶動，固收業務下滑；投行業務年增15%，
主要受到諮詢費收入成長所帶動；非利息費用支出
年增12%，效率比率變差，呆帳與準備金皆較去年
同期減少。公司上調2026年淨利息收入預估，並預

期2027年進一步成長，反映資產負債表擴張及高利
率環境利多。管理層認為美國消費與企業投資仍具
韌性，惟通膨、升息、財政赤字及地緣政治風險仍
是後續觀察重點。
《花旗：上調2026利收預期、下調壞帳率、對潛在
風險謹慎》
3Q26營收年增8%、每股盈餘年增53%。淨利息收
入成長14%，受惠服務、財富管理、市場及信用卡
業務擴張；非利息收入下滑4%；市場收入受益於股
票收入成長；投資銀行收入受益於股權資本市場，
部份被併購諮詢與債權資本市場下滑所抵消；公司
增加壞帳準備金反映了管理潛在經濟風險的謹慎態
度；公司預期第四季市場收入將出現典型的季節性
下滑。公司上調2026年淨利息收入與股東權益報酬
率預期，下修壞帳率。管理層看好美國消費與企業
活動韌性，認為高利率環境及AI投資需求可持續支
撐成長。

Document 501: コモディティ・レポート（2026年7～9月）

Source PDF: all report pdf/コモディティ・レポート（2026年7～9月）.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 37
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 20 日

調査レポート

コモディティ・レポート（2026 年 7～9 月）

調査部 主任研究員 芥田 知至 
1. コモディティ市況全般：原油が上昇傾向、銅は最高値更新、金は上値重い 
ドル建て国際商品市況全般の動向を示す FTSE・コアコモディティ CRB 指数は、2023 年 5 月 31 日に 2022
年 1 月以来の安値をつけた後、2026 年 3～9 月には大幅に上昇して 9 月 10 日に 2008 年 7 月以来の高値を
つけた。その後、10 月にかけてやや下落した。コモディティ相場は、中東情勢や米金融政策の行方を巡って、引
き続き、不安定な推移が見こまれる。

2. エネルギー市況：足元はブレントが一時 110 ドル、WTI が 100 ドル前後 
国際指標のブレント原油は、2026 年 4 月 30 日に 126.41 ドルと 2022 年 3 月以来の高値をつけた。足元は
110 ドル前後で不安定な動きとなっている。米国とイランは戦闘終結やホルムズ海峡再開に向けた動きは停滞し
たままであり、緊迫する中東情勢を受けて原油は高止まりが見こまれる。

3. ベースメタル市況：銅は 9 月上旬に 14,875 ドルの史上最高値 
銅相場は、2026 年 9 月 10 日に 14,875 ドルと史上最高値を記録し、足元は 14,000 ドル台半ばを中心に推
移している。銅は、原油高や景気悪化の影響が懸念される一方で、AI・エネルギー転換・防衛の関連分野を中
心に需要が伸びるとの見方が根強く、高値圏での一進一退が見こまれる。

4. 貴金属市況：金は 8 月中旬に 4,700 ドル近くまで持ち直すも足元は 4,100 ドル前後 
金は、2026 年 1 月 29 日に 5,594.82 ドルと史上最高値を記録した後、6 月 30 日には 3,942.99 ドルまで下
落し、足元は 4,100 ドル前後を中心に推移している。米利上げが続くとの観測が有力な中、中東情勢も安定化に
はまだ遠く、原油高が意識されて、金は買われにくい状況が続きやすいとみられる。

1. コモディティ市況全般の概況：原油が上昇傾向、銅は最高値更新、金は上値重い 
ドル建て国際商品市況全般の動向を示す FTSE・コアコモディティ CRB 指数（終値ベース）は、2022 年 6 月
9 日に 2011 年 9 月以来の高値をつけた後、2023 年 5 月 31 日には 2022 年 1 月以来の安値をつけた。2026
年 3～9 月に大幅に上昇し、9 月 10 日に 2008 年 7 月以来の高値をつけた。10 月にかけてやや下落した。 
マクロ経済環境をみると、フーシ派とサウジアラビアによる攻撃の応酬が激しさを増すなど中東の混乱に広がり
がみられ、米国とイランも戦闘終結やホルムズ海峡再開へ向けた動きは停滞したままである。原油は緊迫する中
東情勢を受けて上昇傾向で推移した。金は、原油高などを受けた根強い米利上げ観測を背景に上値が重い状
況が続いた。銅は、原油高や景気悪化が懸念される一方で、AI・エネルギー転換・防衛の関連分野を中心に需
要が伸びるとの見方が根強く、史上最高値圏で一進一退となった。中東情勢や米金融政策を巡って、コモディテ
ィ相場は不安定な推移が続くと見こまれる。
